- **chronon-api-rate-limit-hit-mid-backfil-e26b09bc1b6b5f02**: Poll the backfill status with growing intervals and stop hammering the status endpoint, resuming from the last known job state, because the monitor itself exhausted the API rate limit by polling a long backfill too aggressively and then could not read the status it needed. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon API rate limit hit mid-backfill monitor error agent failed to poll status. Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-api-rate-limit-hit-mid-backfil-e26b09bc1b6b5f02))
- **chronon-gi**: Unified light transport using the chronon coupling function κ(r). Use when implementing global illumination, rendering, path tracing, or any light transport system. The coupling function IS the Green's function derived from the Matryoshka-Chronon temporal shell framework—it encodes ALL transport in ONE evaluation. Replaces separate calculations for direct light, indirect bounces, shadows, caustics, SSS, and volumetrics. Triggers on requests for GI implementations, real-time rendering, path tracers, or physically-based light transport. ([jenkinsm13/physics-research-dump](https://github.com/jenkinsm13/physics-research-dump/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-gi))
- **chronon-group-by-backfill-failed-error-8921ae9b7a2a3caf**: Confirm the source table actually holds rows for that partition date, then rerun the backfill for just that partition, because the staging query produced no output either because the upstream partition landed late or because the partition filter in the conf points at a date with no source rows. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon group by backfill failed error "staging query produced no output for partition". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-group-by-backfill-failed-error-8921ae9b7a2a3caf))
- **chronon-join-failed-error-left-source-34acd0389d4bdf10**: Deduplicate the left source on the join keys plus the partition column before the join runs, keeping the latest row per key by event timestamp, because Chronon joins require the left side to be unique on its keys and duplicate keys make the point-in-time result ambiguous. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon join failed error "left source has duplicate keys for join". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-join-failed-error-left-source-34acd0389d4bdf10))
- **chronon-upload-failed-error-conf-file-987751bb78163951**: Add at least one group_by to the join conf and run the conf validation again before uploading, because a join with no group_bys has no features to compute and the validator rejects it at upload rather than letting a featureless join deploy. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon upload failed error "conf file validation failed: join has no group_bys". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-upload-failed-error-conf-file-987751bb78163951))

Trigger when the user wants to plan their day or week, schedule tasks, create a daily timetable, or asks how to organize today's work — especially phrases like "help me plan my day," "schedule my tasks," "what should I do today," "plan my week," or "make me a daily plan." - **chronoroot2-segmentation-eval**: Evaluates deep learning models for simultaneous multi-organ segmentation in 2D plant images. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate root systems and aerial parts across different plant species and light conditions, while preserving structural fidelity for downstream phenotypic analysis. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Arabidopsis thaliana 2D phenotyping dataset, Tomato 2D phenotyping dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Dice coefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/chronoroot2-segmentation-eval) · ★3)
- **chronos**: Set up, check, pause, or remove Chronos on Windows and explain its privacy controls.
